Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The homological variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 180 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 244 and intersection form of rank 3.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^244(S, F) is trivial. QED.
